	terra_dw_info_gui = {
		context_type = player_context
		window_name = "terra_dw_info"
		parent_window_token = politics_tab
		visible = {
			check_variable = { CLM_dw_open = 1 }
			check_variable = { CLM_dw_sel > 0 }
		}
		ai_enabled = {
			always = no
		}
		properties = {
			iw_ic = {
				image = "[GetDwSelIcon]"
			}
		}
		triggers = {
			iw_go_click_enabled = {
				OR = {
					AND = { check_variable = { CLM_dw_sel = 1 } check_variable = { CLM_dw_n1 = 1 } }
					AND = { check_variable = { CLM_dw_sel = 2 } check_variable = { CLM_dw_n2 = 1 } }
					AND = { check_variable = { CLM_dw_sel = 3 } check_variable = { CLM_dw_n3 = 1 } }
					AND = { check_variable = { CLM_dw_sel = 4 } check_variable = { CLM_dw_n4 = 1 } }
				}
				OR = {
					AND = { check_variable = { CLM_dw_slot1 = 0 } check_variable = { CLM_dw_sf1 = 1 } }
					AND = { check_variable = { CLM_dw_slot2 = 0 } check_variable = { CLM_dw_sf2 = 1 } }
					AND = { check_variable = { CLM_dw_slot3 = 0 } check_variable = { CLM_dw_sf3 = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
		effects = {
			iw_close_click = {
				set_variable = { CLM_dw_sel = 0 }
			}
			iw_go_click = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { CLM_dw_slot1 = 0 }
						check_variable = { CLM_dw_sf1 = 1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { CLM_dw_sel = 1 }
						}
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_slot1 = 1 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_sp1 = CLM_dw_p1 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_sc1 = 120 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_n1 = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { CLM_dw_sel = 2 }
						}
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_slot1 = 2 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_sp1 = CLM_dw_p2 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_sc1 = 35 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_n2 = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { CLM_dw_sel = 3 }
						}
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_slot1 = 3 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_sp1 = CLM_dw_p3 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_sc1 = 70 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_n3 = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { CLM_dw_sel = 4 }
						}
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_slot1 = 4 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_sp1 = CLM_dw_p4 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_sc1 = 120 }
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_n4 = 2 }
					}
					set_variable = { CLM_dw_sf1 = 2 }
					set_variable = { CLM_dw_sb1 = 1 }
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { CLM_dw_sc1 > 0 }
						}
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_sb1 = CLM_dw_sp1 }
						multiply_variable = { CLM_dw_sb1 = 10 }
						divide_variable = { CLM_dw_sb1 = CLM_dw_sc1 }
						round_variable = CLM_dw_sb1
						add_to_variable = { CLM_dw_sb1 = 1 }
					}
					set_variable = { CLM_dw_cf1 = 5 }
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { CLM_dw_cell1 = 1 }
						}
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_cf1 = CLM_dw_n1 }
						add_to_variable = { CLM_dw_cf1 = 1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { CLM_dw_cell1 = 2 }
						}
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_cf1 = CLM_dw_n2 }
						add_to_variable = { CLM_dw_cf1 = 1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { CLM_dw_cell1 = 3 }
						}
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_cf1 = CLM_dw_n3 }
						add_to_variable = { CLM_dw_cf1 = 1 }
					}
					if = {

							check_variable = { CLM_dw_cell1 = 4 }
						}
						set_variable = { CLM_dw_cf1 = CLM_dw_n4 }
